new milestone in partnership with LAB\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eSunu has been proudly partnered with the Louisiana Association for the Blind, LAB since 2018. Our partnership began with a small initial order and a visit to LAB headquarters in Shreveport Louisiana. Since our first visit, LAB has become a great partner and ally in our mission. Since then, LAB has places recurring orders for Sunu Band. We delighted to announce that we've closed our largest recurring order deal, for 600 units - marking a significant milestone in our partnership which will extend the follow:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eLAB will be distributing Sunu Band within four states, including Louisiana, in the Southern USA.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eLAB will include Sunu Band and our navigation app within its rehabilitation training with Sunu's official certified training.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eLAB will be model for training experts and specialists on Sunu's technology.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003ch3\u003eAbout Louisiana Association for the Blind\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eL.A.B. is an affiliate of National Industries for the Blind, L.A.B. participates in the AbilityOne Program, a federal initiative to help people who are blind or have other severe disabilities find employment with nonprofit agencies that sell products and/or services to the United States government. Louisiana Association for the Blind has been shining a light for people with visual impairments through training, services and employment since 1927. L.A.B. offers vision evaluations, children’s programs, independent living skills instruction, job training and employment placement through our Community Services fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eBeyond willpower: the business of empowering people\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eEvery startup has a unique story. The founders are the human-side to every venture and endeavor. Sometimes, the founder's story is overlooked beyond all the hype. Today, we're proud to share our story with you - featured on Republic's Superheroes Journal. \u0026nbsp;The editor's interview is a great opportunity for you to know the personal and human side of Sunu. \u0026nbsp;Here are some quick excerpts from our \u003ca href=\"https://republic.co/blog/marco-trujillo-and-dr-fernando-albertorio-sunu\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003efull interview\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eWhere did the name “Sunu” come from?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe name Sunu is derived from a Mayan word that means ‘colibri’ or hummingbird. The logo represents the hummingbird and our colors represent positivity, willpower and determination. Our branding is inspired on our mission to empower independence, freedom of mobility and harnessing and enhancing human willpower and determination through technology. \u0026nbsp; \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eWhat is your superpower?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFernando\u003c/strong\u003e: My superpower is empathy. I’m naturally empathetic and have found ways to apply my empathy in product design, user experience, user research and sales/business development.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMarco\u003c/strong\u003e: My superpower is willpower. Many times along my personal and entrepreneurial journey, I’ve faced challenges that seemed impossible at a glance, but one way or the other, I managed to get through. In these moments, my logic, knowledge or emotions didn’t provide a solution.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eWhat's the team culture like?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMarco\u003c/strong\u003e: Sunu’s core values are courage, empathy and gratitude. These values drive all aspects of our culture towards our ultimate shared goal: that people with disabilities are truly embraced and fully integrated in our society. Some examples of how we create this culture is by setting goals with deep learnings (stretch quarterly goals, massive action sprints, learning debriefs and team retrospectives), maintaining transparent communication at all levels (work dynamics feedback, company progress, personal performance), caring about individual well-being (well-being monitoring, open discussions/keynotes, perks for personal development), empathy development (volunteering visits to centers, conversations with customers, sensibilization activities), and more.\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3 style=\"text-align: center;\"\u003eClick below for the full story\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ch3 style=\"text-align: center;\"\u003e\u003ca href=\"https://republic.co/blog/marco-trujillo-and-dr-fernando-albertorio-sunu\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/021/680/21680-1592844238-8d21948c24f7e34c767e83c94823cc3af02f13a2.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\" style=\"width: visually impaired worldwide. Close of 90% of visually impaired people live in developing countries. India is home to the world's largest population, with \u003cstrong\u003e47.7 million people\u003c/strong\u003e who are blind. India represents one of largest potential market segments for Sunu. And throughout the past month, our team has been working on creating partnerships within the country and we've launched our first pilot in India.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe purpose of this multiphase pilot program is to:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eUnderstand the market in India.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u0026nbsp;Validate the kinds of business opportunity/business model that Sunu can pursue in the country.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eEstablish our business case that allow us to address this market segment.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eEstablish a pathway / go-to-market to successfully introduce the product in India.\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe're already having traction, and have:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003eA team in India who is co-managing the pilot.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eSunu Bands in the country.\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003eForming partnerships with organizations for the blind and ready for user testing.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe pilot in India, which is addressing a significant market segment will accelerate Sunu as a business and its global impact. The results from the pilot will allow Sunu to replicate this program in other countries - therefore allow us to capture a large segment of the global market.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eFinally, we thank Guarav Mishra for his support as a value-add investor in id=\"offerings_update_form_2385_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn our \u003ca href=\"https://republic.co/sunu-fernando-albertorio-featured-on-cnn-coping-with-disease-and-disability-in\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eApril 6th update\u003c/a\u003e, we shared how Sunu is leading the conversation on enabling people with disabilities to thrive during COVID-19 as reported on CNN Health. We've continue our work in this important area as now more countries, states and cities are opening up - there is still a lot concern, stress and anxiety among people who are out in public spaces. Now social distancing is part of our 'new normal'. Public health officials, government and the private sector are pouring a lot of resources into ensuring that p\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/021/520/21520-1592315427-1476bb37de2dd8255bb874d08959f2d7a92703a7.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-fil fr-dii\" style=\"width: 274px;\"\u003eeople in public places remain at least 6 feet (2 meters) apart. The use of 'contactless technology' is having a surge in applications from payments, security, entertainment, health screening, and will continue to enter into our everyday lives as social distancing practices are expected to be the norm for the foreseeable future. The \u003ca href=\"https://www.bizjournals.com/boston/news/2020/06/15/3-local-companies-working-on-contactless-tech.html?iana=hpmvp_bost_news_headline\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eBoston Business Journal\u003c/a\u003e selected Sunu among three companies whose technology will help people maintain social distancing. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eUsing Sunu Band to know your proximity to obstacles, \u003cstrong\u003eincluding objects and people\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003ethat are within the environment \u003cstrong\u003eis at the core of our technology\u003c/strong\u003e. Sunu Band can detect people up to 16 feet (5.5 meters) away. Then haptic vibrations simply inform the user when they're getting closer. So using Sunu Band to \u003cstrong\u003epractice social distancing is simply another user-case\u003c/strong\u003e of our technology. And some of our blind and low vision users have already been reporting that Sunu Band is helping them maintain social distancing. That said, Sunu Band has the potential to capture the interest of a much broader group of people (and potential market). Perhaps Sunu Band can help those individuals who are immune-compromised at ensuring that they practice proper social distancing, relieving their stress and anxiety when out in public. Sunu Band may also be potentially useful in the service and hospitality industries. But regardless of its application, Sunu Band remains as a technology and tool that improves quality of life. Be sure to check out our short video below to learn more.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan class=\"fr-video fr-fvc fr-dvb fr-draggable\"\u003e\u003ciframe name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_2325_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eOn Friday May 29, photojournalist and author \u003ca href=\"https://www.nytimes.com/2020/05/30/us/minneapolis-protests-press.html\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eLinda Tirado\u003c/a\u003e was covering a protest in Minneapolis when she was shot in the eye by a rubber bullet used by police. In less than 24 hours and after a surgery - Linda lost her left eye and now is now blind on that side. With her life turned around and now visually impaired, Linda is finding ways to cope. Late at night, as I read her story and the \u003ca href=\"https://www.dallasnews.com/news/2020/06/03/a-dallas-man-says-he-lost-his-eye-in-a-protest-now-he-wants-chief-hall-to-find-those-responsible/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003estories\u003c/a\u003e of three other individuals who have lost an eye and their sight (in less than 24 hours) due to the use of rubber bullets - I felt that I need to do something and couldn't stay on my couch. So I got on social media and reach out to Linda, and others. After a call with Linda, it became very clear -\u003cstrong\u003e\u0026nbsp;I am announcing that Sunu will provide help and relief by gifting a Sunu Band to people who are suffering through the trauma of eye injuries and blindness due to actions and use of non-lethal devices for crowd control at peaceful protests.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/021/101/21101-1591460487-0c7f81fd427a56ef0d5234c0b416eaa6a000eaf1.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dii fr-fil\" style=\"width: 197px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLinda will be receiving a Sunu Band next week along with connections and support to organizations groups and professionals in the blind and visually impaired community who will be helping her along her new life journey. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eA new danger in the air\u0026nbsp;\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eA new danger is in the air that can leave anyone blind -- rubber bullets, paint balls, tear gas, all mechanisms used by the police for crowd control. Life-altering eye injuries are a common result of urban warfare and rioting. We have seen it around the world and we now see it in the United States. In just 24 hours, I've read about 3 people who have lost an eye because of \u003ca href=\"https://www.fastcompany.com/90512287/the-lethal-history-of-rubber-bullets-and-why-they-should-never-be-used-on-peaceful-protesters\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003erubber bullets\u003c/a\u003e. Americans have the right to speak and congregate publicly and should be able to exercise that right without the fear of blindness.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eSIX WAYS TO PROTECT YOUR EYES DURING A PROTEST OR WHEN RUBBER BULLETS ARE USED AGAINST THE CROWD.\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eI also \u003ca href=\"http://www.fernandoalbertorio.com/six-ways-to-protect-your-eyes-during-a-protest-or-when-rubber-bullets-are-used-against-the-crowd/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003epublished an article on my blog\u003c/a\u003e based on my research on how best people can protect their eyes (and themselves) if they participate in a peaceful protest, march or rally and in the event that they encounter police action and the use of rubber bullets, tear gas, pepper balls and sprays. These form of 'non-lethal' devices can cause sever harm to the eyes.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eHow can you help?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eMy personal mission is making the world more accessible. As WE lift up our voices for justice, to end racism, inequality and oppression, everyone of us are 'able'. Please consider sharing these articles and help raise public awareness. If you are able, please consider increasing your investment in Sunu by $25, $50, or $100. Or you prefer to donate a Sunu Band to someone else like Linda, please reach out to me directly at fernando@sunu.com.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 s-borderTop1 u-displayFlex name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_2258_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eImagine walking down the sidewalk on the way to work or the store, and suddenly finding yourself turned around because new construction work is blocking your route. Or suddenly hitting your head against scaffolding that wasn't there yesterday. Have you ever found yourself in an unfamiliar area, trying to glance at your map app to decide which street to get on... then you realizing that the crosswalk signal doesn't work? These may be a minor inconvenience to most sighted people. But for those of us who are blind and low vision, these represent some of the frequent occurrences that effect our ability to navigate our surroundings and live independently. Our new mobile app intends to enhance the navigation experience for blind and low vision people.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/020/611/20611-1590244175-5be8c3d859710c6e18f6534cb1dc26d46c5680e4.gif\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs shown in our product roadmap, our mobile app product is being developed in three phases. We've already achieved phase 1, and we're currently testing via an invited beta despite its launch being delayed by the quarantine orders because of coronavirus.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur second phase will allow Sunu app to become the Waze for the visually impaired traveler. With the features that we are developing along with the feedback that we're already gaining through our extended trails - blind pedestrians will know when there is an obstruction ahead, like construction or anything that limits access to their route. Our mobile app will also provide accessibility information about places. Through crowdsourced information, our app will inform users if a place has large print, information in braille, or report those places that are not friendly to guide dogs. \u0026nbsp;\u003cstrong\u003eSo what does our product roadmap have to do with google?\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/020/612/20612-1590244745-908c77416c18ff1fb5a76d7af07027b2a46e6050.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\" style=\"width: 391px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3 style=\"text-align: left;\"\u003eGoogle Maps will highlight accessible locations with a wheelchair icon\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis is great news! It not only validates Sunu's product roadmap for the mobile app;. it also means that Google is putting more attention towards accessibility within Google Maps. Google is introducing a new Accessible Places feature to Maps and search that the company says will make it easier for wheelchair users to see if a location they want to visit is accommodating to their needs. When you enable the functionality, you'll see a wheelchair icon whenever a business or public venue has an accessible entrance. You'll also be able to see if it has accessible seating, washrooms and or parking. What's more, if a destination doesn't have an entrance that is accommodating to different mobility needs, Maps will more explicitly show that information.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWe're making moves to enhance our use of Google's Maps API's and data sources. We're leveraging our connections within Y-Combinator to further gain credits and access within Google Cloud and the Maps team.\u003c/strong\u003e In the meantime, check out the video below to learn more.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cspan class=\"fr-video fr-fvc fr-dvb fr-draggable\"\u003e\u003ciframe src=\"https://www.youtube.com/embed/-oWsAMwJ-ks?\u0026amp;wmode=opaque\" frameborder=\"0\" This week, we began our collaboration with New Products \u0026amp; Technology Committee at the Tucson Arizona VA Blind Rehabilitation Center, which will be testing out the Sunu Band and our mobile app. We will be providing the VA team with on-boarding and virtual training throughout the next couple of weeks. \u0026nbsp;This is an important steps towards attaining vendor status with the VA healthcare system.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/019/029/19029-1587054304-ddd368867c3f1c326f953d23b0ede5521fd6a03e.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003ch3\u003eWhat is the VA?\u003c/h3\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe Veterans Health Administration is the first national healthcare system to completely integrate rehabilitation services for Veteran and active Service-members with visual impairments into its health benefits. There are more than one million US Veterans who are blind and visually impaired. The VA provides blind and low vision rehabilitation services as well as cutting-edge assistive technology throughout its blind and low vision clinics, outpatient clinics and blind rehabilitation id=\"offerings_update_form_1908_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eWe hope that you are doing well, staying healthy and safe. Our shared reality is sending shockwaves through each of our hearts, minds, families, and communities. \u0026nbsp;There is so much happening so quickly, so many emotions, so many hoops to jump through to keep ourselves and the people around us safe. Our team is leaning into the challenge. We are committed to the safety and wellbeing of our employees, our customers, partners, stakeholders and everyone in the community. We are committed to amplifying the potential of our technology to solve problems, address critical needs, and facilitate human connection at this time of crisis, especially for those most vulnerable among us. Here is how our team is managing through the COVID-19 coronavirus time:\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTeam\u003c/strong\u003e: Our team in Boston and Guadalajara are well, and have been working remotely from home. We’re a remote first company - meaning that working remotely has been a practice that’s infused into our company’s culture and communication infrastructure.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eProduct Development\u003c/strong\u003e: We decided to delay the release of our new navigation app because of the recent public health orders for people to remain at home in order to reduce the spread of coronavirus. We are still going to release our private beta, but mostly to onboard our partners to the new features.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe've also shifted our focus to accelerate our R\u0026amp;D initiatives (Sunu Band 2) \u0026nbsp;and optimize product quality before the release. We continue to make progress in the development of our next generation hardware. We have now selected a firm that will provide the prototyping, validation and manufacturing of our new hardware.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBusiness Development\u003c/strong\u003e: We canceled business trips and events as we focus on engaging with our partners worldwide remotely. We continue to manage deals in our pipeline. We’re also providing new online content for prospecting new clients.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDuring this time where everyone is working remotely, we are focusing on training our partners and we’re developing a virtual Sunu Certification program with some of our leading partners. This certification will provide valid Continuous Education Credits (CEUs) to specialists, foster our relationship with the specialists who prescribe the product for subsidies and extend the value of our partners as they will be able to provide an official training that they charge at an hourly rate. Putting these strategies into place now will help us accelerate and scale our B2B sales when the health crisis returns to normal.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eManufacturing \u0026amp; Production\u003c/strong\u003e: We currently maintain inventory to manage various orders within our pipeline and still fulfill online orders. We’re in close contact with our distribution center, ShipBob, FedEx and UPS, and have adjusted for shipping delays during this time.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur manufacturing manager, Adrian Ramos, has 20+ years experience managing production when he was at P\u0026amp;G. He successfully managed the largest production plant in Mexico during the H1N1 epidemic and has implemented a lot of the practices, procedures and safeguard to our production facility.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFinally\u003c/strong\u003e: We wish to thank all of our investors on Republic for their continued support. It’s important to remember the long-term view of investing in early-stage companies. Please stay tuned to our updates for important news and be sure to watch our semifinal episode on Meet the Drapers.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWe hope you stay well, healthy and be safe.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFernando, Marco \u0026amp; Team Sunu.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv
